's_' + o.t : 'patch ' + (o.i + 1) + ' of z_' + o.t; } var col = { act: '--k-act', state: '--k-state', patch: '--k-ctx' }; var inst = {}; inst.draw = function () { var g = ctx.g, w = ctx.w, hh = ctx.h, toks = tokens(), L = toks.length; g.clearRect(0, 0, w, hh); var lab = 22, pad = 10, size = Math.min((w - lab - pad * 2) / L, (hh - lab - pad * 2 - 18) / L); var x0 = (w - size * L - lab) / 2 + lab, y0 = pad + lab; geom = { x0: x0, y0: y0, size: size, L: L, toks: toks }; var soft = u.css('--line'), allowC = u.css('--k-ema'), selC = u.css('--k-pred'), ink3 = u.css('--ink-3'), line = u.css('--ink-2'); for (var i = 0; i < L; i++) { var ai = ctx.vis(toks[i].k); g.globalAlpha = ai; g.fillStyle = u.css(col[toks[i].k]); g.fillRect(x0 - lab + 4, y0 + i * size + 1, lab - 8, Math.max(1, size - 2)); g.fillRect(x0 + i * size + 1, y0 - lab + 4, Math.max(1, size - 2), lab - 8); for (var j = 0; j < L; j++) { var ok = toks[j].t <= toks[i].t, a = Math.min(ai, ctx.vis(toks[j].k)); g.globalAlpha = a * (ok ? (sel === -1 ? 0.55 : sel === i ? 1 : 0.18) : 1); g.fillStyle = ok ? (sel === i ? selC : allowC) : soft; g.fillRect(x0 + j * size + 0.5, y0 + i * size + 0.5, Math.max(0.5, size - 1), Math.max(0.5, size - 1)); } } g.globalAlpha = 1; g.strokeStyle = line; g.lineWidth = 1.2; for (var t = 0; t <= T; t++) { var q = t * (n + 2) * size; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x0 + q, y0); g.lineTo(x0 + q, y0 + L * size); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x0, y0 + q); g.lineTo(x0 + L * size, y0 + q); g.stroke(); } g.font = '500 10px ' + u.css('--mono'); g.fillStyle = ink3; for (t = 0; t < T; t++) g.fillText('t=' + t, x0 + (t + 0.5) * (n + 2) * size - 10, y0 + L * size + 13); g.fillText('keys →', x0, 12); g.save(); g.translate(10, y0 + 40); g.rotate(-Math.PI / 2); g.fillText('queries →', 0, 0); g.restore(); var real = T * 258, frac = (T + 1) / (2 * T); read.textContent = 'drawn ' + L + ' tokens · real ' + fmt(real) + ' tokens at T = ' + T + ' · ' + Math.round(100 * frac) + '% of entries allowed' + (sel >= 0 ? ' · row: ' + name(toks[sel]) : ''); }; function cellAt(x, y) { if (!geom) return null; var i = Math.floor((y - geom.y0) / geom.size), j = Math.floor((x - geom.x0) / geom.size); if (i < 0 || i >= geom.L || j < 0 || j >= geom.L) return null; return [i, j]; } inst.pick = function (x, y) { var c = cellAt(x, y); if (!c) return null; var q = geom.toks[c[0]], k = geom.toks[c[1]], ok = k.t <= q.t; return { title: 'query ' + name(q) + ' → key ' + name(k), shape: ok ? 'allowed: block ' + k.t + ' ≤ block ' + q.t : 'masked: key is in the future', body: q.k === 'patch' && k.k === 'act' && k.t === q.t ? 'This is how the action enters: patches of step t read a_t, and their output predicts frame t + 1.' : '' }; }; inst.click = function (x, y) { var c = cellAt(x, y); sel = c ? (sel === c[0] ? -1 : c[0]) : -1; inst.draw(); }; function sync() { T = parseInt(inT.value, 10); n = parseInt(inN.value, 10); byId('vj-bc-T-v').textContent = T; byId('vj-bc-n-v').textContent = n; sel = -1; inst.draw(); } inT.addEventListener('input', sync); inN.addEventListener('input', sync); T = parseInt(inT.value, 10); n = parseInt(inN.value, 10); return inst; }); /* =================================================================== 10. Teacher forcing versus rollout =================================================================== */ VJ.def3d('rollout', function (ctx) { var THREE = ctx.THREE, V = THREE.Vector3; var step = ctx.stage.closest('.step'); ctx.setView(new V(0, 0, 0), 12.5, 0.28, 1.05); var gGt = ctx.part('gt', 'Ground-truth latents z_k', 'E(x_k), LayerNormed; 256 × 1408 each', '--k-ema'); var gTf = ctx.part('tf', 'Teacher-forced predictions', 'ẑ_(k+1) = P(z_≤k): every step supervised', '--k-pred', new V(0, 0, 1)); var gRo = ctx.part('roll', 'Rollout predictions', 'ẑ_(k+1) = P(ẑ_≤k): steps 1 and 2 supervised', '--k-pred', new V(0, 0, -1)); var gBand = ctx.part('band', 'Error band', '‖e_k‖ ≤ ε(L^k − 1)/(L − 1), schematic constants', '--warn', new V(0, 0, -1)); var inH = byId('vj-ro-H'), inE = byId('vj-ro-e'), inL = byId('vj-ro-L'); var mGt = ctx.mat('--k-ema'), mPr = ctx.mat('--k-pred'), mSup = ctx.mat('--warn'), mBand = ctx.mat('--warn', { opacity: 0.18, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }); var mArr = ctx.mat('--ink-3'), mCur = ctx.mat('--accent'); var S = { H: 8, e: 0.1, L: 1.1, mode: 'both' }, cursor = null, cursorT = 0, info = null; var lblTf = ctx.label('teacher forcing', new V(), { part: 'tf', cls: 'lbl--strong' }); var lblRo = ctx.label('rollout', new V(), { part: 'roll', cls: 'lbl--strong' }); var lblSup = ctx.label('rollout loss: T = 2', new V(), { part: 'roll', cls: 'lbl--warn' }); var lblEnd = ctx.label('', new V(), { part: 'band', cls: 'lbl--warn' }); function err(k) { return Math.abs(S.L - 1) < 1e-6 ? S.e * k : S.e * (Math.pow(S.L, k) - 1) / (S.L - 1); } function build() { [gGt, gTf, gRo, gBand].forEach(clearGroup); var H = S.H, dx = Math.min(0.9, 9 / H), x0 = -dx * H / 2, sc = 1.3, zT = 1.7, zR = -1.7; function gtp(k, z) { return new V(x0 + k * dx, 0.55 * Math.sin(0.7 * k), z); } var clipped = false; [zT, zR].forEach(function (z) { for (var k = 0; k <= H; k++) { var b = ctx.box(0.26, 0.26, 0.26, mGt); b.position.copy(gtp(k, z)); gGt.add(b); if (!k) ctx.tip(b, 'z_' + k + ' (ground truth)', 'E(x_k): (256, 1408)'); } }); var prevR = gtp(0, zR); var top = [], bot = []; for (var k2 = 1; k2 <= H; k2++) { var pT = gtp(k2, zT).add(new V(0, (k2 % 2 ? 1 : -1) * S.e * sc, 0)); var bT = ctx.box(0.22, 0.22, 0.22, mPr); bT.position.copy(pT); gTf.add(bT); ctx.tip(bT, 'ẑ_' + k2 + ' teacher-forced', 'from the true z_' + (k2 - 1) + '; error stays ≈ ε = ' + S.e.toFixed(2)); var aT = arrowMesh(ctx, mArr, 0.018); aT.userData.set(gtp(k2 - 1, zT), pT.clone().add(new V(-0.12, 0, 0))); gTf.add(aT); var s1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.2, 0.025, 6, 20), mSup); s1.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; s1.position.copy(pT).add(new V(0, -0.22, 0)); gTf.add(s1); var e = err(k2) * sc, eD = Math.min(e, 2.4); if (e > 2.4) clipped = true; var pR = gtp(k2, zR).add(new V(0, eD, 0)); var bR = ctx.box(0.22, 0.22, 0.22, mPr); bR.position.copy(pR); gRo.add(bR); ctx.tip(bR, 'ẑ_' + k2 + ' rolled out', '‖e_' + k2 + '‖ ≤ ' + err(k2).toFixed(3) + (k2 <= 2 ? '; supervised by the rollout loss' : '; never supervised')); var aR = arrowMesh(ctx, mArr, 0.018); aR.userData.set(prevR, pR.clone().add(new V(-0.12, 0, 0))); gRo.add(aR); if (k2 <= 2) { var s2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.2, 0.025, 6, 20), mSup); s2.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; s2.position.copy(pR).add(new V(0, -0.22, 0)); gRo.add(s2); } prevR = pR; } for (var k3 = 0; k3 <= H; k3++) { var c = gtp(k3, zR), r = Math.min(err(k3) * sc, 2.4); top.push(c.clone().add(new V(0, r + 0.02, 0))); bot.push(c.clone().add(new V(0, -r - 0.02, 0))); } var pos = []; for (var i = 0; i < H; i++) { [top[i], bot[i], top[i + 1], bot[i], bot[i + 1], top[i + 1]].forEach(function (p) { pos.push(p.x, p.y, p.z); }); } var bg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); bg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3)); bg.computeVertexNormals(); var band = new THREE.Mesh(bg, mBand); gBand.add(band); ctx.tip(band, 'Error band', 'radius ε(L^k − 1)/(L − 1); ' + (S.L < 1 ? 'saturates at ε/(1 − L) = ' + (S.e / (1 - S.L)).toFixed(2) : 'grows geometrically')); lblTf.anchor = new V(x0 - 0.6, 0.9, zT); lblRo.anchor = new V(x0 - 0.6, 0.9, zR); lblSup.anchor = gtp(1.5, zR).add(new V(0, -0.8, 0)); lblEnd.anchor = gtp(H, zR).add(new V(0.5, Math.min(err(H) * sc, 2.4) + 0.35, 0)); lblEnd.el.textContent = '‖e_' + H + '‖ ≤ ' + err(H).toFixed(2) + (clipped ? ' (drawn clipped)' : ''); cursor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.1, 12, 8), mCur); gRo.add(cursor); info = { gtp: gtp, zR: zR, H: H }; ctx.parts.forEach(function (p) { p.group.userData.home.copy(p.group.position); }); applyMode(); calc(); mark(); } function applyMode() { gTf.visible = S.mode !== 'roll'; gRo.visible = gBand.visible = S.mode !== 'tf'; lblTf.hidden = S.mode === 'roll'; lblRo.hidden = lblSup.hidden = lblEnd.hidden = S.mode === 'tf'; mark(); } function calc() { var eH = err(S.H); byId('vj-ro-calc').innerHTML = u.tile(S.e.toFixed(2), 'teacher-forced error per step') + u.tile(eH.toFixed(2), 'rollout bound at step ' + S.H, eH > 3 * S.e) + u.tile((eH / S.e).toFixed(1) + '×', 'rollout vs one-step error', eH > 3 * S.e) + u.tile(S.L < 1 ? (S.e / (1 - S.L)).toFixed(2) : 'none', 'saturation ε/(1 − L)') + u.tile('2 of ' + S.H, 'rollout steps with a training signal', S.H > 2); } function sync() { S.H = parseInt(inH.value, 10); S.e = parseFloat(inE.value); S.L = parseFloat(inL.value); byId('vj-ro-H-v').textContent = S.H; byId('vj-ro-e-v').textContent = S.e.toFixed(2); byId('vj-ro-L-v').textContent = S.L.toFixed(2); build(); } [inH, inE, inL].forEach(function (c) { c.addEventListener('input', sync); }); onSeg(step, '[data-ctl="ro-mode"]', function (v) { S.mode = v; applyMode(); }); sync(); return { update: function (dt) { if (!info || dt <= 0) return false; cursorT = (cursorT + dt * 1.2) % (info.H + 1); var k = Math.floor(cursorT), f = cursorT - k, a = info.gtp(k, info.zR), b = info.gtp(Math.min(info.H, k + 1), info.zR); var ea = Math.min(err(k) * 1.3, 2.4), eb = Math.min(err(Math.min(info.H, k + 1)) * 1.3, 2.4); cursor.position.copy(a.lerp(b, f)).add(new V(0, ea + (eb - ea) * f, 0.3)); return true; } }; }); /* =================================================================== 11. Energy landscape and CEM =================================================================== */ VJ.def3d('cem', function (ctx) { var THREE = ctx.THREE, V = THREE.Vector3; var step = ctx.stage.closest('.step'); ctx.setView(new V(0, 0.3, 0), 6.2, 0.55, 0.95); var gSurf = ctx.part('surf', 'Energy surface E(Δx, Δy)', 'schematic, scaled to Fig. 9 (0.40 to 0.44 over ±5 cm)', '--k-goal'); var gSmp = ctx.part('smp', 'Samples', 'N action samples this iteration', '--ink-3'); var gEl = ctx.part('elite', 'Elites', 'top k by energy; the Gaussian is refit to them', '--k-pred'); var gGs = ctx.part('gauss', 'Sampling Gaussian', '2σ ellipse of the plotted mean action', '--k-act'); var gMk = ctx.part('marks', 'Minimum and true action', 'surface minimum; Fig. 9 true action (0, −10) cm', '--k-goal'); var gBox = ctx.part('box', 'Action box', 'each coordinate clipped to ±7.5 cm', '--ink-2'); var HALF = 0.075, K = 20, HS = 1.7; var land = 'smooth', S = { N: 800, k: 10, I: 10, H: 1 }, cem = null, running = false, acc = 0; function Eraw(dx, dy) { if (land === 'smooth') return 0.40 + 0.032 * ((dx / 0.1) * (dx / 0.1) + ((dy + 0.05) / 0.1) * ((dy + 0.05) / 0.1)); var a = (dx + 0.03) * (dx + 0.03) + (dy - 0.035) * (dy - 0.035), b = (dx - 0.045) * (dx - 0.045) + (dy + 0.05) * (dy + 0.05); return 0.44 - 0.022 * Math.exp(-a / (2 * 0.025 * 0.025)) - 0.04 * Math.exp(-b / (2 * 0.009 * 0.009)) + 0.01 * ((dx / 0.1) * (dx / 0.1) + (dy / 0.1) * (dy / 0.1)); } function E(mx, my) { return Eraw(S.H * mx, S.H * my); } var eMin = 0.39, eMax = 0.48; function hgt(e) { return (e - eMin) / (eMax - eMin) * HS; } function sc(m) { return m * K; } var surfMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.75, metalness: 0, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.92 }); surfMat.userData.base = 0.92; var wireMat = ctx.lineMat('--ink-3', { opacity: 0.18 }); var surf = null, wire = null; function buildSurf() { clearGroup(gSurf); var R = 48, geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2 * sc(HALF), 2 * sc(HALF), R, R); geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); var p = geo.attributes.position, cols = [], vals = [], lo = 9, hi = -9, i; for (i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { var e = E(p.getX(i) / K, p.getZ(i) / K); vals.push(e); lo = Math.min(lo, e); hi = Math.max(hi, e); } eMin = lo - 0.002; eMax = Math.max(hi, lo + 0.03); var cA = VJ.color('--k-goal'), cB = VJ.color('--surface-2'); for (i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { p.setY(i, hgt(vals[i])); var f = Math.pow((vals[i] - eMin) / (eMax - eMin), 0.6); var c = cA.clone().lerp(cB, f); cols.push(c.r, c.g, c.b); } geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(cols, 3)); geo.computeVertexNormals(); surf = new THREE.Mesh(geo, surfMat); gSurf.add(surf); surf.userData.tipFn = function (hit) { var x = hit.point.x / K, y = hit.point.z / K; return { title: 'E(Δx, Δy) = ' + E(x, y).toFixed(3), shape: 'mean action (' + (100 * x).toFixed(1) + ', ' + (100 * y).toFixed(1) + ') cm' + (S.H > 1 ? ', total ×' + S.H : ''), body: 'Schematic surface. Low is closer to the goal representation.' }; }; wire = new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.WireframeGeometry(geo), wireMat); gSurf.add(wire); clearGroup(gMk); var best = [0, 0], be = 9; for (var a = -HALF; a <= HALF + 1e-9; a += HALF / 60) for (var b = -HALF; b <= HALF + 1e-9; b += HALF / 60) { var ee = E(a, b); if (ee < be) { be = ee; best = [a, b]; } } var mMin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.09), ctx.mat('--k-goal')); mMin.position.set(sc(best[0]), hgt(be) + 0.12, sc(best[1])); gMk.add(mMin); ctx.tip(mMin, 'Surface minimum', '(' + (100 * best[0]).toFixed(1) + ', ' + (100 * best[1]).toFixed(1) + ') cm, E = ' + be.toFixed(3)); if (land === 'smooth') { var gt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.08, 14, 10), ctx.mat('--ink')); gt.position.set(0, 0.05, sc(-0.1 / S.H)); gMk.add(gt); ctx.tip(gt, 'True action, Fig. 9', '(0, −10) cm' + (S.H > 1 ? ', i.e. (0, −' + (10 / S.H).toFixed(1) + ') cm per step at H = ' + S.H : ': outside the ±7.5 cm box')); } VJ.cemBest = { x: best[0], y: best[1], e: be }; mark(); } var boxMat = ctx.lineMat('--ink-2', { opacity: 0.8 }); gBox.add(new THREE.LineLoop(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new V(-sc(HALF), 0.01, -sc(HALF)), new V(sc(HALF), 0.01, -sc(HALF)), new V(sc(HALF), 0.01, sc(HALF)), new V(-sc(HALF), 0.01, sc(HALF))]), boxMat)); ctx.label('Δx →', new V(sc(HALF) + 0.25, 0, 0), {}); ctx.label('Δy', new V(0, 0, sc(HALF) + 0.25), {}); var gtLbl = ctx.label('true action (0, −10) cm', new V(0, 0.3, -2.0), { part: 'marks' }); var MAXD = 800; var smp = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.03, 6, 5), ctx.mat('--ink-3'), MAXD); var eli = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.055, 10, 8), ctx.mat('--k-pred'), 50); gSmp.add(smp); gEl.add(eli); smp.userData.tipFn = function () { return { title: 'Sample', shape: 'one candidate action sequence, scored by one predictor rollout' }; }; eli.userData.tipFn = function () { return { title: 'Elite', shape: 'one of the top ' + S.k + ' samples; mean and std are refit to these' }; }; var ellMat = ctx.lineMat('--k-act', { opacity: 1 }); var ell = new THREE.LineLoop(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(new Array(49).fill(0).map(function () { return new V(); })), ellMat); gGs.add(ell); var meanM = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.07, 12, 8), ctx.mat('--k-act')); gGs.add(meanM); ctx.tip(meanM, 'Sampling mean μ', 'the action CEM would execute if it stopped now'); function newCem() { var d = 2 * S.H, mu = [], sd = []; for (var i = 0; i < d; i++) { mu.push(0); sd.push(0.05); } cem = { it: 0, mu: mu, sd: sd, r: rng(1234), best: 9, pts: [], el: [] }; cem.g = gaussFn(cem.r); draw(); } function iterate() { if (!cem || cem.it >= S.I) return false; var d = 2 * S.H, all = []; for (var n = 0; n < S.N; n++) { var a = [], mx = 0, my = 0; for (var j = 0; j < d; j++) { var v = Math.max(-HALF, Math.min(HALF, cem.mu[j] + cem.sd[j] * cem.g())); a.push(v); } for (var h = 0; h < S.H; h++) { mx += a[2 * h] / S.H; my += a[2 * h + 1] / S.H; } all.push({ a: a, x: mx, y: my, e: E(mx, my) }); } all.sort(function (p, q) { return p.e - q.e; }); var el = all.slice(0, S.k); for (j = 0; j < d; j++) { var m = 0, s = 0; el.forEach(function (p) { m += p.a[j] / el.length; }); el.forEach(function (p) { s += (p.a[j] - m) * (p.a[j] - m) / Math.max(1, el.length - 1); }); cem.mu[j] = m; cem.sd[j] = Math.max(1e-4, Math.sqrt(s)); } cem.best = Math.min(cem.best, el[0].e); cem.pts = all; cem.el = el; cem.it++; draw(); return true; } function draw() { var m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), z0 = new THREE.Matrix4().makeScale(0.0001, 0.0001, 0.0001), i; for (i = 0; i < MAXD; i++) { var p = cem.pts[i]; if (p && i < MAXD) { m4.makeTranslation(sc(p.x), hgt(p.e) + 0.03, sc(p.y)); smp.setMatrixAt(i, m4); } else smp.setMatrixAt(i, z0); } for (i = 0; i < 50; i++) { var q = cem.el[i]; if (q) { m4.makeTranslation(sc(q.x), hgt(q.e) + 0.06, sc(q.y)); eli.setMatrixAt(i, m4); } else eli.setMatrixAt(i, z0); } smp.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; eli.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; var mx = 0, my = 0, vx = 0, vy = 0; for (var h = 0; h < S.H; h++) { mx += cem.mu[2 * h] / S.H; my += cem.mu[2 * h + 1] / S.H; vx += cem.sd[2 * h] * cem.sd[2 * h] / (S.H * S.H); vy += cem.sd[2 * h + 1] * cem.sd[2 * h + 1] / (S.H * S.H); } var pos = ell.geometry.attributes.position; for (i = 0; i < 49; i++) { var th = i / 48 * 6.2832, ex = mx + 2 * Math.sqrt(vx) * Math.cos(th), ey = my + 2 * Math.sqrt(vy) * Math.sin(th); var cx = Math.max(-HALF, Math.min(HALF, ex)), cy = Math.max(-HALF, Math.min(HALF, ey)); pos.setXYZ(i, sc(cx), hgt(E(cx, cy)) + 0.04, sc(cy)); } pos.needsUpdate = true; meanM.position.set(sc(mx), hgt(E(mx, my)) + 0.1, sc(my)); var b = VJ.cemBest || { x: 0, y: 0, e: 0 }, dist = Math.hypot(mx - b.x, my - b.y) * 100; byId('vj-cem-calc').innerHTML = u.tile(cem.it + ' / ' + S.I, 'iteration') + u.tile(cem.best < 9 ? cem.best.toFixed(4) : '–', 'best energy found') + u.tile('(' + (100 * mx).toFixed(1) + ', ' + (100 * my).toFixed(1) + ') cm', 'mean action μ') + u.tile(dist.toFixed(1) + ' cm', 'μ to surface minimum', cem.it >= S.I && dist > 1.5) + u.tile(fmt(S.N * S.I * S.H), 'predictor calls per action') + u.tile((100 * S.k / S.N).toFixed(2) + '%', 'elite fraction k/N') + u.tile(2 * S.H + '-D (model 7H)', 'search dimension here'); mark(); } function reset() { running = false; byId('vj-cem-run').textContent = 'Run CEM'; buildSurf(); newCem(); } ctx.onRecolor = function () { buildSurf(); if (cem) draw(); }; function sync() { S.N = parseInt(byId('vj-cem-n').value, 10); S.k = Math.min(parseInt(byId('vj-cem-k').value, 10), S.N); S.I = parseInt(byId('vj-cem-it').value, 10); S.H = parseInt(byId('vj-cem-h').value, 10); byId('vj-cem-n-v').textContent = S.N; byId('vj-cem-k-v').textContent = S.k; 'Pause CEM' : 'Run CEM'; }); byId('vj-cem-step').addEventListener('click', function () { if (cem.it >= S.I) newCem(); iterate(); }); sync(); var shown = false; return { onShow: function () { if (!shown) { shown = true; if (!VJ.reduced) { running = true; byId('vj-cem-run').textContent = 'Pause CEM'; } else { iterate(); } } }, update: function (dt) { if (!running) return false; acc += dt; if (acc > 0.55 || (dt > 0 && VJ.reduced)) { acc = 0; if (!iterate()) { running = false; byId('vj-cem-run').textContent = 'Run CEM'; } } return true; } }; }); /* =================================================================== 12. The MPC loop on the robot =================================================================== */ VJ.def3d('mpc', function (ctx) { var THREE = ctx.THREE, V = THREE.Vector3; var step = ctx.stage.closest('.step'); ctx.setView(new V(-0.1, 0.7, 0), 7.8, 0.35, 1.05); var gTab = ctx.part('table', 'Table', 'unseen lab, not to scale', '--ink-3'); var gArm = ctx.part('arm', 'Arm', 'executes one action per step, blocking', '--ink-2'); [[g1, 4], [g2, 10], [g3, 4]] : [[g3, 18]]; } function reset() { ep = { k: 0, phase: 'plan', t: 0, tip: new V(-0.1, 0.9, 0.3), from: null, to: null, closed: false, holding: false, cupPos: A.clone(), E: [], done: false, plan: 0, wait: 0 }; ep.sched = goals(); arm.pose(ep.tip, true); cup.position.copy(ep.cupPos); hud(); calc(); mark(); } function goalAt(k) { var acc = 0; for (var i = 0; i < ep.sched.length; i++) { acc += ep.sched[i][1]; if (k < acc) return { g: ep.sched[i][0], i: i }; } return { g: ep.sched[ep.sched.length - 1][0], i: ep.sched.length - 1 }; } function energy(g) { var dT = ep.tip.distanceTo(g.tip), dC = ep.cupPos.distanceTo(g.name.indexOf('grasp') !== -1 ? A : (g.name.indexOf('near') !== -1 ? g.tip.clone().add(new V(0, -0.05, 0)) : Bp)); return 0.40 + 0.018 * dT + 0.03 * dC + (g.close !== ep.closed ? 0.004 : 0); } var hudBox = ctx.hud(''); function hud() { var gi = goalAt(Math.min(ep.k, 17)).i, names = ep.sched.map(function (s) { return s[0].name; }); var spark = ''; if (ep.E.length > 1) { var lo = Math.min.apply(null, ep.E) - 0.002, hi = Math.max.apply(null, ep.E) + 0.002; spark = '<svg width="120" height="30" viewBox="0 0 120 30" aria-hidden="true"><polyline fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" points="' + ep.E.map(function (e, i) { return (i / 17 * 118 + 1).toFixed(1) + ',' + (28 - (e - lo) / (hi - lo) * 26).toFixed(1); }).join(' ') + '"/></svg>'; } hudBox.innerHTML = '<div class="hudbox">' + names.map(function (n, i) { return (i === gi ? '<span class="hot">▶ ' : '<span>') + n + '</span>'; }).join('<br>') + '</div>' + '<div class="hudbox" style="color:var(--k-goal)"><b>energy per step</b><br>' + (spark || 'planning…') + '</div>'; } function calc() { var per = S.plan === 'vj' ? 16 : 240, so = ep.k * per, tot = 18 * per; function mmss(s) { var m = Math.floor(s / 60), r = Math.round(s % 60); return m + ' min ' + (r < 10 ? '0' : '') + r + ' s'; } var outcome = !ep.done ? 'running' : (ep.holding || ep.cupPos.distanceTo(Bp) < 0.25) && ep.cupPos.distanceTo(Bp) < 0.25 ? 'success' : 'failure: cup not moved'; byId('vj-mpc-calc').innerHTML = u.tile(Math.min(ep.k + (ep.done ? 0 : 1), 18) + ' / 18', 'planning step') + u.tile(mmss(so), 'planning time so far') + u.tile(mmss(tot), 'episode total at ' + per + ' s/step', tot > 3600) + u.tile(ep.E.length ? ep.E[ep.E.length - 1].toFixed(3) : '–', 'energy after last step') + u.tile(outcome, 'outcome', outcome.indexOf('failure') === 0); } onSeg(step, '[data-ctl="mpc-plan"]', function (v) { S.plan = v; calc(); }); subIn.addEventListener('change', function () { S.sub = subIn.checked; reset(); }); byId('vj-mpc-reset').addEventListener('click', reset); reset(); var rC = rng(77); function update(dt) { if (dt <= 0) return false; if (ep.done) { ep.wait += dt; if (ep.wait > 2.5) reset(); return true; } ep.t += dt; var gi = goalAt(ep.k), g = gi.g; goalM.position.copy(g.tip).add(new V(0, 0.25, 0)); var m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), z0 = new THREE.Matrix4().makeScale(0.0001, 0.0001, 0.0001); if (ep.phase === 'plan') { var shrink = 1 - Math.min(1, ep.t / 0.8); for (var i = 0; i < 60; i++) { m4.makeTranslation(ep.tip.x + (rC() - 0.5) * 0.7 * shrink + 0.05, ep.tip.y + (rC() - 0.5) * 0.5 * shrink, ep.tip.z + (rC() - 0.5) * 0.7 * shrink); cloud.setMatrixAt(i, m4); } cloud.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; if (ep.t > 0.8) { ep.phase = 'exec'; ep.t = 0; var d = new V().subVectors(g.tip, ep.tip), L = d.length(), stepL = 0.34; ep.from = ep.tip.clone(); ep.to = L > stepL ? function camPos(deg) { var r = 3.3, a = deg * Math.PI / 180; return new V(0.2 - r * Math.cos(a), 2.2, -r * Math.sin(a)); } var TXT = { camera: ['The camera now sits at a different angle. The model worked out which way "left" is from one camera position; from another, its idea of the directions is rotated, so every move goes a little sideways. It still gets there, by a curved path.', 'App. B.4: for camera positions 35° to 85°, the rotation between inferred and executed actions grows almost linearly with angle; the mean absolute error is about 1.6 cm on 5 cm moves. Replanning keeps the distance falling at every step, but not as fast as it could, which matches Fig. 8. The slope drawn here is schematic.', 'The error is a rotation because the fitted 2 × 2 map has condition number ≈ 1.5. That makes the fix cheap: rotate planned actions by W⋆ estimated from random motions. The deeper problem is that the action frame is inferred from pixels at all; any camera that moves, as on a humanoid, re-opens it every step.'], horizon: ['Give the planner only the final photo and it takes the shortest route to looking like it: the empty gripper goes to the plate. Nothing in a one-step plan rewards the detour to pick up the cup first.', 'With horizon 1 the planner chooses the action that most lowers the distance to the goal image now. Moving the gripper toward its final pose lowers it; grasping the cup first raises it for several steps. This is why the paper supplies two sub-goal images for pick-and-place (App. B.2) and names long-horizon planning as a limitation (§4.3).', 'A longer horizon helps only if the rollout stays accurate that long and the search finds the non-greedy sequence in a 7H-dimensional space with a fixed sample budget. ' + a[3][j].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + a[3][j].slice(1) + '.</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>'; } function setMode(m) { mode = m; armG.visible = m === 'arm'; handG.visible = m === 'hand'; humG.visible = m === 'humanoid'; var I = INFO[m]; byId('vj-emb-calc').innerHTML = u.tile(I.dim, 'action space') + u.tile(I.rate, 'control rate needed', m !== 'arm') + u.tile(I.cam, 'camera', m !== 'arm'); list(); mark(); } onSeg(step, '[data-ctl="emb"]', setMode); setMode('arm'); function pose(tt) { if (mode === 'arm') { arm.pose(new V(0.3 + 0.25 * Math.sin(tt * 0.8), 0.45 + 0.1 * Math.sin(tt * 1.3), 0.3 * Math.cos(tt * 0.8)), true); cam.position.set(-0.4, 1.9, 2.2); cam.lookAt(new V(0.2, 0.4, 0)); } else if (mode === 'hand') { var wob = 0.25 * Math.sin(tt * 0.9); handG.rotation.set(0, wob, 0.15 * Math.sin(tt * 0.7)); fingers.forEach(function (f, i) { var flex = 0.35 + 0.35 * Math.sin(tt * 1.6 + i * 0.7), p = f.base.clone(), dir = f.thumb ? new V(0.5, 0.5, -0.7).normalize() : new V(0, 0.35, 1).normalize(); f.segs.forEach(function (s, k) { var len = f.thumb ? 0.2 : 0.24 - k * 0.03; var q = p.clone().addScaledVector(dir, len); setSeg(s, p, q); p = q; dir.applyAxisAngle(new V(1, 0, 0), -flex * 0.7).normalize(); }); }); var cp = new V(0, 1.75, -0.2).applyAxisAngle(new V(0, 1, 0), wob); cam.position.copy(cp); cam.lookAt(new V(0, 1.2, 0.8).applyAxisAngle(new V(0, 1, 0), wob)); } else { var ph = tt * 3.2, bob = 0.04 * Math.abs(Math.sin(ph)); torso.position.set(0, 1.55 + bob, 0); var yaw = 0.5 * Math.sin(tt * 0.6); head.position.set(0, 2.3 + bob, 0); var hip = [new V(-0.2, 1.05 + bob, 0), new V(0.2, 1.05 + bob, 0)], sh = [new V(-0.45, 1.95 + bob, 0), new V(0.45, 1.95 + bob, 0)]; [0, 1].forEach(function (s) { var sw = (s ? 1 : -1) * 0.45 * Math.sin(ph); var knee = hip[s].clone().add(new V(0, -0.5, 0.5 * Math.sin(sw))), foot = knee.clone().add(new V(0, -0.5 + 0.08 * Math.max(0, Math.sin(ph + s * Math.PI)), 0.2 * Math.sin(sw) - 0.05)); setSeg(limbs[s * 2], hip[s], knee); setSeg(limbs[s * 2 + 1], knee, foot); var el = sh[s].clone().add(new V((s ? 0.1 : -0.1), -0.45, -0.3 * Math.sin(sw))), hd = el.clone().add(new V(0, -0.2, 0.35)); setSeg(limbs[4 + s * 2], sh[s], el); setSeg(limbs[5 + s * 2], el, hd); }); var fwd = new V(Math.sin(yaw), -0.35, Math.cos(yaw)).normalize(); cam.position.set(0, 2.38 + bob, 0).addScaledVector(fwd, 0.18); cam.lookAt(cam.position.clone().add(fwd)); } axes.position.copy(cam.position).add(new V(0, 0.35, 0)); axes.quaternion.copy(cam.quaternion); camLbl.anchor = cam.position.clone().add(new V(0, 0.55, 0)); camLbl.el.textContent = mode === 'arm' ? 'camera fixed' : 'camera moves: action frame changes'; } pose(0); return { update: function (dt) { t += dt; pose(t); return dt > 0; } }; }); if (VJ.boot) VJ.boot(); })(); </script>
